- **principle**: DRY（Don't Repeat Yourself） ：避免重复的代码，尽量将相同的逻辑提取到函数或模块中。 KISS（Keep It Simple, Stupid） ：保持设计简单，避免不必要的复杂性。 YAGNI（You Aren't Gonna Need It） ：只实现当前需要的功能，不要提前实现可能不会用到的功能。 SOLID原则： 单一职责原则（SRP）：一个类应该只有一个原因引起变化。 开放-关闭原则（OCP）：软件实体应该对扩展开放，对修改关闭。 里氏替换原则（LSP）：子类型必须能够替换掉它们的父类型。 接口隔离原则（ISP）：不应该强迫客户依赖它们不使用的方法。 依赖反转原则（DIP）：高层模块不应该依赖低层模块，二者都应该依赖抽象。 ([sjzsdu/wn](https://github.com/sjzsdu/wn/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/principle.md) · ★2)
- **principle**: Reviews code for conventions, consistency, scalability, DRY, SOLID, and strict TypeScript. Strongly prefers framework / package / module configuration over patching. Catches abstractions that should be config and configurations that should be code. ([imrim12/openclaw.edu.vn](https://github.com/imrim12/openclaw.edu.vn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/principle.md))
- **principle**: Reviews code for conventions, consistency, scalability, DRY, SOLID, and strict TypeScript. Enforces Nuxt 4 layer architecture, NuxtHub import patterns, and project-specific hard rules. Prefers framework/package config over custom workarounds. Use post-implementation for code review before a PR is marked ready. ([imrim12/skills](https://github.com/imrim12/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/principle.md))

- **principles-reviewer**: Read-only review of a diff or codebase slice against DRY, SOLID, service-boundary, and repo-convention violations, then hands remediation to the matching doing agent (scout/researcher/implementer/debugger/reviewer, or a domain-specific one). Does not edit files, does not implement fixes, no style nits. ([lgulliver/pi](https://github.com/lgulliver/pi/blob/HEAD/agents/principles-reviewer.md))
- **principles-reviewer**: Fast, independent, read-only review of a code change against the programming principles and the Karpathy guidelines, covering correctness counterexamples, design and tests in one bounded pass. Use before presenting a non-trivial change, or when the user asks for a review. Give it the base commit (or "uncommitted"), the intent, and any acceptance examples. Name its requirements lens, or give a plan with acceptance criteria, to have the change checked against them too. ([piotr-yuxuan/shift-left](https://github.com/piotr-yuxuan/shift-left/blob/HEAD/plugins/principles/agents/principles-reviewer.md))
- **principles-reviewer**: Reviews this project's unreviewed changes against its principles (docs/alpha/principles.toml), its specification (docs/alpha/specification.md) and its process (CONTRIBUTING.md), and for bugs, security and data loss, scale at the expected load, missing tests, slowness and code that shouldn't exist, reporting each problem against the rule or lens it falls under. Run it when a push to main or a pull request merge asks for a review, or when the user asks. ([vs/alpha](https://github.com/vs/alpha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/principles-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **print**: Turns content into a clean, dense, publication-grade PDF. Use whenever the user wants a PDF made — from a markdown file, notes, a report, a document draft, a live URL, or an HTML file — or says "print this", "make a PDF", "PDF this page", "export to PDF". Renders, measures actual page fill, and retunes density to kill sparse pages. Never hand over a PDF that has not been measured. ([cercit/AI-Skills](https://github.com/cercit/AI-Skills/blob/HEAD/agents/print.md))
- **print-advisor-architect**: Read-only architecture investigator for the MentorSlicer Print Advisor. Use before implementing a Print Advisor change to verify existing APIs, call flow, thread/lifecycle constraints and the smallest integration boundary. ([MirkoUgoliniDev/MentorSlicer](https://github.com/MirkoUgoliniDev/MentorSlicer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/print-advisor-architect.md))
- **print-advisor-native**: Implements C++ changes to the MentorSlicer Print Advisor (local API server, routes, confirmation dialogs, profile copies, printer commands, monitor, telemetry, engine options). Use for native-side Print Advisor work; use print-advisor-sidecar for the TypeScript MCP sidecar. ([MirkoUgoliniDev/MentorSlicer](https://github.com/MirkoUgoliniDev/MentorSlicer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/print-advisor-native.md))
